# Aegis complete skill and candidate register

Pinned source: `9ab6aeac252c2bf10e86d06b24fd9feacf55b024`. This register accompanies **Audit of all Aegis skills**.

## How to read the statuses

All 205 packages were fully read. The seven columns are trigger, overlap, duplication/extension, eval integrity, substance, scope and invocation posture. **P** means no additional confirmed static concern; **C** means a documented concern; **F** means a static instruction/fixture failure under the review rubric. None is a live model behavior result. Each JSON row retains the complete source reviewer assessment, evidence and rationale.

## Complete source identity and review records

The JSON contains all 792 file hashes, all seven check records for every skill, all 300 original reviewer rows, contextual dispositions for all 86 scanner candidates, source checks, external-source purposes and the full role/family inventory. The evidence archive retains the complete independent domain reports, freeze records and cross-reviews.
